# Skills · C · part 177

808 entries, copy-pagina-de-servicos-e-link-da-bio to cor-de-cinema. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **copy-pagina-de-servicos-e-link-da-bio**: Criar o texto completo de uma página de serviços — seja site, Notion público, link da bio (Stan.store, Linktree, etc.) ou qualquer página de apresentação — que transforma visitantes em clientes com uma narrativa clara de quem você atende, qual problema resolve, como funciona e por que contratar você. Resolve a diferença entre "ter uma página" e "ter uma página que vende". ([tiagopgr/skills-ia](https://github.com/tiagopgr/skills-ia/tree/HEAD/skills/marketing/skills/copy-pagina-de-servicos-e-link-da-bio) · ★42 · 2 copies)
- **copy-panel-rewrite**: Rewrites every string a person reads in a product in plain language, then three blind research-backed judge panels decide each change (two of three wins); verdicts are applied, tested, committed and reported in one HTML page. Use on /copy-panel-rewrite, "rewrite all our copy in plain language", "let judges decide the wording", "copy sweep before release". ([michaelezehi/nw-skills](https://github.com/michaelezehi/nw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/writing/copy-panel-rewrite))

- **copy-pass**: 统一 UI/物品/系统短文案的术语、长度与语气。触发：文案、copy、UI文案、物品描述。 ([limoaCatherine/game-studio-harness](https://github.com/limoaCatherine/game-studio-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/copy-pass) · ★4)
- **copy-pass**: Rewrite page copy slots against BRAND.md voice lock — headlines, support, CTAs. Use when copy feels off-brand, placeholder, or the user asks for a copy edit. ([Alfred-Lau/skills](https://github.com/Alfred-Lau/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/design/copy-pass) · ★2)
- **copy-paste**: Disciplined workflow for taking a found UI reference, component, or code snippet (from ui-research, a screenshot, or a link the user pasted) and adapting it into the project correctly — instead of pasting it in wholesale. Use whenever inspiration or example code from outside the project is about to be integrated into it, or when the user pastes a component/snippet and asks to "use this" or "make it look like this." ([sysv86/kromite](https://github.com/sysv86/kromite/tree/HEAD/kromite-ui/copy-paste) · ★3)
- **copy-paste-boxes**: Use this when the user wants copy-paste posts, emails, or drafts in separate boxes. ([Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates](https://github.com/Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates/tree/HEAD/templates/community/personal-admin/psycho/skills/copy-paste-boxes) · ★6)
- **copy-paste-fehler-erkennung**: Für Copy-Paste-Fehler erkennen: ordnet Norm, Beweislast und Gegenargument; Ergebnis: Prüfprodukt mit Risiko und nächstem Schritt. ([ThomasMoreAI/legal-skills-open](https://github.com/ThomasMoreAI/legal-skills-open/tree/HEAD/de/general/skills/copy-paste-fehler-erkennung) · ★136)
- **copy-paste-fehler-erkennung**: Wenn es um Copy-Paste-Fehler erkennen in Plugin: status-navigator-step-plan geht: ordnet Akteninhalt, Belege, Lücken und Nachforderungen; liefert eine Schnittstellenkarte mit Kollisions-, Zuständigkeits- und Nachweisfragen. ([vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim](https://github.com/vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim/tree/HEAD/status-navigator-step-plan/skills/copy-paste-fehler-erkennung))
- **copy-paste-friendly**: ユーザーにコード変更や手順を提示する時、コピー操作が複数のコードブロックに 分割されて何度もコピペさせる事態を防ぐスキル。理想は「1回コピーして手元で 実行したら全変更が適用される単一スクリプト」にまとめること。同一ファイルへの 複数箇所の置換を別々の ``` ブロックに割らない。 トリガー:「コードを提示」「置換指示」「コピペ」「コピー分かれてる」「手元で当てる」 「パッチ」「diff」「複数箇所の編集を渡す」「手元の repo に貼る」「触れない repo の修正手順」 等。Claude が repo に直接 push できず、ユーザーが手元で適用する時に必ず使用。 ([ippoan/claude-skills](https://github.com/ippoan/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/copy-paste-friendly))
- **copy-paste-via-file**: Whenever the assistant needs the user to copy-paste a command, code snippet, prompt, or any text where chat-rendered formatting could mangle line wrapping, regex literals, or string contents (e.g. DevTools console one-liners, terminal commands with quoted JSON, long URLs, multi-line prompts), write the exact text to a file and open it so the user can copy it clean. Use whenever the assistant is about to say "paste this command into..." or similar, especially for browser DevTools console pastes, complex shell commands, or anything containing regex literals, escaped quotes, or backticks. ([cobuchan/compounding-operator](https://github.com/cobuchan/compounding-operator/tree/HEAD/skills/copy-paste-via-file))
- **copy-pattern-replacement**: 当用户要求直接根据页面上可见的文案，把它替换为指定的图案、Unicode 符号、emoji 或已有本地素材时使用。无需知道源码文件；用于低成本、精确的单点替换，不用于重设计、批量改文案或创建新图标。 ([Ferrystd/awesome-lostsky](https://github.com/Ferrystd/awesome-lostsky/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/copy-pattern-replacement))
- **copy-pipeline**: End-to-end copywriting pipeline backtested against real data. Given a copy task (landing page, email sequence, VSL, DM sequence, ad), pulls sales call transcripts and objections from Supabase, loads best-performing copy examples, brand voice, and relevant data into NotebookLM for grounded analysis, then writes copy informed by what actually closes deals. Triggers on "write copy for [X]", "copy pipeline [X]", "write a [landing page/email/VSL/DM/ad]", or any copywriting request. ([chriswestt/claude-skills](https://github.com/chriswestt/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/copy-pipeline) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **copy-plain-ko**: Audit and rewrite Korean product/intro-site copy so it reads as written by a person, not an LLM, and uses 개조식 (terse noun-ending lines) where site/AGENTS.md allows it. Use when asked to check copy for "AI 티", "GPT 말투", "번역투", "문구 검사", "카피 리뷰", "개조식으로", "서술형 줄이기", or a "copy audit" of site/*.html, menu labels, or release notes. ([hjung3113/analytics-platform](https://github.com/hjung3113/analytics-platform/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/copy-plain-ko))
- **copy-planner**: Plan content briefs — audience, goals, messaging, success criteria. Prevents rewrites and scope drift before drafting. ([zivtech/joyus-desktop](https://github.com/zivtech/joyus-desktop/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/copy-planner))
- **copy-polish**: Polishes and tightens existing marketing/website copy without rewriting it: sharpens clarity, voice, proof, specificity, and CTAs. Detects the language and applies the right passes — English "Seven Sweeps", German "Lektorat" that removes translationese. Use whenever the user wants to improve, edit, tighten, "make better", review, lektorieren, or fix existing copy (heroes, landing pages, feature blurbs, emails) in English or German. German triggers: "mach den Text besser", "lektorieren", "Text überarbeiten", "klingt übersetzt", "Copy verbessern", "entkrampfen". English triggers: "polish this",… ([bndkts/copycraft](https://github.com/bndkts/copycraft/tree/HEAD/skills/copy-polish) · ★1)
- **copy-polish**: Sweep every user-visible string and text deliverable for AI-writing tells, topic-only titles, and register drift.
